_Protects how an NPC describes (and locates) third parties the player names — relation thresholds and the kin/clan/faction gating of gossip. These run on every name-bearing message and break in silence: a drift would have lords mislabel friends as strangers, or omnisciently place everyone._

- GIVEN a spouse, WHEN categorizing, THEN kinship wins over any relation score
- GIVEN a parent or child, WHEN categorizing, THEN each kinship is named
- GIVEN deep regard, WHEN categorizing, THEN they are a very close friend
- GIVEN warmth at the friend threshold, WHEN categorizing, THEN they are a friend
- GIVEN deep hostility, WHEN categorizing, THEN they are a bitter enemy
- GIVEN mild hostility at the enemy threshold, WHEN categorizing, THEN they are an enemy
- GIVEN a neutral clanmate, WHEN categorizing, THEN clan membership is named
- GIVEN two same turf gang leaders at neutral standing, WHEN categorizing, THEN they are named rivals not strangers
- GIVEN rival gang leaders who happen to hold regard, WHEN categorizing, THEN the rivalry overrides the friendly label
- GIVEN rival gang leaders who are genuine enemies, WHEN categorizing, THEN the stronger enemy label is kept
- GIVEN a faint acquaintance outside any clan, WHEN categorizing, THEN they are an acquaintance
- GIVEN a stranger, WHEN categorizing, THEN they are not personally known
- GIVEN relation scores across the range, WHEN describing, THEN each tier is named
- GIVEN two people in the same settlement, WHEN judging knowledge, THEN it is certain
- GIVEN close kin or clan, WHEN judging knowledge, THEN it is confident regardless of relation
- GIVEN only a friendly bond, WHEN judging knowledge, THEN it is recent hearsay
- GIVEN no personal tie but a shared faction, WHEN judging knowledge, THEN only rough movements are known
- GIVEN a distant stranger, WHEN judging knowledge, THEN nothing is known
- GIVEN no knowledge, WHEN building a whereabouts clause, THEN there is none
- GIVEN present together, WHEN building a whereabouts clause, THEN it names the shared place
- GIVEN a known person whose place is unreadable, WHEN building a clause, THEN honest uncertainty is voiced
- GIVEN each knowing tier, WHEN building a clause, THEN the framing matches the tie
- GIVEN a woman, WHEN naming her gender, THEN it reads a woman
- GIVEN a man, WHEN naming his gender, THEN it reads a man
- GIVEN this npc holds the prisoner, WHEN clausing, THEN it is present tense and forbids a false backstory
- GIVEN the player holds the prisoner, WHEN clausing, THEN it names the player
- GIVEN a third party holds the prisoner, WHEN clausing, THEN it names the captor
- GIVEN a third party holds the prisoner but the captor is unknown, WHEN clausing, THEN it says someone else
- GIVEN the person is free, WHEN clausing, THEN there is no clause

### TraitBandTests.cs

_Pins how a Bannerlord trait level (the small signed integer hero.GetTraitLevel returns) becomes the plain band word "low"/"moderate"/"high"._

- GIVEN a negative level, WHEN describing, THEN it reads low
- GIVEN a zero level, WHEN describing, THEN it reads moderate
- GIVEN a positive level, WHEN describing, THEN it reads high

### TruncationFallbackPolicyTests.cs

_Documents the PROSE FALLBACK decision (Prose + Interpreter composition): whether a beat the prose model just produced should be redone, in one INTEGRATED call, on the configured fallback (grok) model. The prose call is issued with the SDK's internal double-budget retry disabled so a truncated reply returns immediately; this is what decides, from that reply's finish reason alone, whether the fast failure earns the fallback._

- GIVEN a length finish reason, WHEN a fallback model is configured, THEN it falls back
- GIVEN a content filter finish reason, WHEN a fallback model is configured, THEN it falls back
- GIVEN a null finish reason, WHEN a fallback model is configured, THEN it falls back
- GIVEN a stop finish reason, WHEN a fallback model is configured, THEN it does not fall back
- GIVEN a clean stop but no spoken dialogue, WHEN a fallback model is configured, THEN it falls back
- GIVEN no spoken dialogue, WHEN no fallback model is configured, THEN it does not fall back
- GIVEN a clean stop with a spoken line, WHEN a fallback model is configured, THEN it does not fall back
- GIVEN a truncated finish reason, WHEN no fallback model is configured, THEN it does not fall back

### TrystPolicyTests.cs

_Guards TrystPolicy (Inc 3b-a): whether a partner who has taken a lover MEETS him this week. A tryst may happen only when she has a lover, the player is not beside her (an unobserved meeting), and a modest weekly roll lands. This is the hook the wife-to-lover bond growth, the secret-affair discovery curve, and the later pregnancy-by-the-lover build on. Get it wrong and trysts fire while the player rides at her side (she meets her lover under the husband's nose, which the design forbids), or never fire at all (the whole 3b/3c line is dead), or fire for a partner with no lover (a tryst with no one)._

- GIVEN a partner with no lover, WHEN asked, THEN no tryst
- GIVEN the player is beside her, WHEN asked, THEN no tryst
- GIVEN a lover and the player away, WHEN the roll lands under the chance, THEN they meet

### UndeliverableReasonDescriberTests.cs

_Documents the failure-report wording: which cause an undeliverable letter resolves to, and the exact line the player reads. Before this batch every failure said "could not be reached", so the player never learned that the recipient DIED or was CAPTURED mid-flight (the courier robustness batch, ROADMAP 2026-07-20). The wording is load-bearing: it is the only channel through which the player learns the fate of a letter's recipient._

- GIVEN a dead recipient, WHEN the failure is described, THEN the player learns of the death
- GIVEN a captured recipient, WHEN the failure is described, THEN the player learns of the captivity
- GIVEN a recipient both dead and flagged prisoner, WHEN resolved, THEN death wins
- GIVEN a vanished recipient, WHEN the failure is described, THEN the wording stays generic

### UnknownActionNoticeTests.cs

_Pins the wording and the diagnostic block for an [ACTION] the bridge refused because its type is not a real mod verb. This is the feedback funnel: the player sees an immersive, deed-scoped line (never "nothing happened", which contradicted a triumphant scene where a +1 regard and a quest DID land that turn), and the invented verb is always logged with its context so the author can add it later. What each channel says is what these tests hold in place._

- GIVEN the base message, WHEN read, THEN it is deed scoped and never says nothing happened
- GIVEN reporting is off, WHEN composing, THEN only the base message shows
- GIVEN reporting is on, WHEN composing, THEN the base message carries a person free report hint
- GIVEN a full context, WHEN formatting the log entry, THEN it captures the verb params npc and lines
- GIVEN nulls and blanks, WHEN formatting the log entry, THEN blank fields are omitted and it never throws

### VassalageOfferPolicyTests.cs

_join_as_vassal fix (player report, 2026-08-05): "asked the legitimate ruler to become her vassal, the LLM narrated acceptance and threw 6000 denars, but the game only ever registered the player as a mercenary." Verifies the two guards the bridge consults before swearing the player's clan into vassalage, so a narrated oath must actually change the player's kingdom standing from mercenary/independent to full vassal, or be surfaced as refused - never a silent no-op that leaves the fiction and the save diverged._

- GIVEN the npc is the kingdom ruler and the player is not already a vassal, WHEN resolving join as vassal, THEN the oath is allowed
- GIVEN the npc is not the kingdom ruler, WHEN resolving join as vassal, THEN the oath is refused
- GIVEN the player is already a full vassal, WHEN resolving join as vassal, THEN the oath is refused
- GIVEN the player is at war with the kingdom, WHEN resolving join as vassal, THEN the oath is refused
- GIVEN the npc is not the ruler and the player is already a vassal, WHEN resolving join as vassal, THEN the oath is refused

### VentriloquizedReactionPolicyTests.cs

_Round-table audit D7. When the player opens the floor, the lap that follows gives each person in the room a real turn in their own voice. The main NPC's [WITNESS_REACTION] lines on that same turn are the model ventriloquizing those same people, so showing them made a witness speak twice in a row, and since the two lines come from two different model calls the second could flatly contradict the first. This policy picks which voice wins. It was an inline condition in ChatViewModel.Exchanges and therefore untestable (the mod is net472, this project is net10)._

- GIVEN the floor is open on the hosts turn, WHEN deciding, THEN the reactions are dropped
- GIVEN no floor request is pending, WHEN deciding, THEN the reactions are kept
- GIVEN a witness and not the host is replying, WHEN deciding, THEN the reactions are kept
- GIVEN a captive scene, WHEN deciding, THEN the reactions are kept
- GIVEN the conversation has already ended, WHEN deciding, THEN the reactions are kept

### VerbRegistryCoreTests.cs

_The pure dispatch/composition engine behind CalradiaRemembers.Verbs.VerbRegistry, which registers BOTH the mod's own conversation verbs and any ExternalVerbAdapter wrapping a third-party mod's ExternalVerbContract in that very same list. So the crash-containment and lookup rules pinned here are what stands between one faulty external verb and every player's conversation window: the v1.21.0 regression (a verb's IsEligible touching a missing game API closed EVERY chat) is exactly the failure mode a hostile or outdated third-party verb could reproduce, by accident or otherwise. Case-insensitive name routing, the null-vs- nothing-composed sentinel, and explicit-order composition are the mod's own dispatch guarantees this class has to keep honest for external verbs too, not just its own._

- GIVEN one eligible and one ineligible verb, WHEN composing teachings, THEN only the eligible one is included
- GIVEN multiple eligible verbs, WHEN composing without an explicit order, THEN they concatenate in registration order
- GIVEN no eligible verbs, WHEN composing teachings, THEN the result is null
- GIVEN an eligible verb whose teaching text is empty, WHEN composing, THEN nothing is appended for it
- GIVEN an explicit order naming a subset, WHEN composing teachings, THEN only those names are composed in that order
- GIVEN an explicit order naming an unregistered verb, WHEN composing teachings, THEN that name is skipped
- GIVEN an ineligible verb that states its door, WHEN composing, THEN it speaks though its teaching is hidden
- GIVEN a verb that chooses silence, WHEN composing, THEN nothing is contributed
- GIVEN a verb not implementing IStatesUnavailability, WHEN composing, THEN it contributes nothing
- GIVEN a verb that throws stating its door, WHEN composing, THEN it is skipped and the others still speak
- GIVEN several verbs stating doors, WHEN composing, THEN they come back in registration order
- GIVEN an ineligible verb with guidance text, WHEN composing guidance, THEN it is included though composing teachings excludes it
- GIVEN a verb not implementing IProvidesGuidance, WHEN composing guidance, THEN it contributes nothing
- GIVEN a guidance callback that throws, WHEN composing guidance, THEN it is skipped and others still compose
- GIVEN no verb offers guidance text, WHEN composing guidance, THEN the result is null
- GIVEN a verb whose eligibility throws, WHEN composing teachings, THEN it is skipped and the others still compose
- GIVEN a faulting verb and a fault sink, WHEN composing teachings, THEN the exception is reported to the sink
- GIVEN an action name matching a registered verb, WHEN executing, THEN that verbs Execute runs and its result is returned
- GIVEN an action name with no matching verb, WHEN executing, THEN the result is null
- GIVEN an action name differing only by case, WHEN executing, THEN it still routes to the matching verb
- GIVEN a registered verb, WHEN unregistered by name, THEN it no longer executes or teaches
- GIVEN a registered verb, WHEN unregistered with a differently cased name, THEN it still removes it
- GIVEN an unknown or blank name, WHEN Unregister is called, THEN it returns false without throwing

### WarDeedMatcherTests.cs

_Guards the declare-war deed: it completes only when the PLAYER declared war on the demanded faction — never when the player was attacked, and never on an unrelated war. A drift would credit (or deny) the deed for the wrong war._

- GIVEN the player declares war on the demanded faction, WHEN judging, THEN it is satisfied
- GIVEN war was declared ON the player, WHEN judging, THEN it is NOT satisfied
- GIVEN war with a different faction, WHEN judging, THEN it is NOT satisfied
- GIVEN case differences, WHEN judging, THEN matching is case insensitive
- GIVEN missing ids, WHEN judging, THEN it is NOT satisfied

### WarLoomingPolicyTests.cs

_Guards WarLoomingPolicy, the gate deciding whether a pair of kingdoms not yet at war is tense enough to record a WarLooming WorldEvent (which then spreads as rumour, "what you've heard", and the chronicle). Its answer feeds the weekly world-conditions scan directly: a wrong "yes" spams the realm with phantom war scares, a wrong "no" leaves the world silent about a war everyone in Calradia would be bracing for. Each boundary below pins one of those failures. Rationale derived from the policy's own header/docs._

- GIVEN the pair is already at war, WHEN deciding, THEN it is not recorded
- GIVEN peace is fresher than the minimum, WHEN deciding, THEN it is not recorded
- GIVEN peace exactly at the minimum age, WHEN all else holds, THEN it is recorded
- GIVEN the war score is below threshold, WHEN deciding, THEN it is not recorded
- GIVEN the war score exactly at threshold, WHEN all else holds, THEN it is recorded
- GIVEN the same pair loomed too recently, WHEN deciding, THEN it is not recorded
- GIVEN the cooldown has exactly elapsed, WHEN all else holds, THEN it is recorded
- GIVEN the pair has never loomed before, WHEN all else holds, THEN it is recorded

### WarWearinessTests.cs

_Documents companion war-weariness: wounds and captures accumulate a toll, rest eases it, Valor sets how much a companion bears before wanting out, and the retirement they ask for depends on whether they hold land._

- GIVEN a battle wound, WHEN applied, THEN the toll rises by one
- GIVEN a capture, WHEN applied, THEN the toll rises by ten
- GIVEN a score near the ceiling, WHEN more toll lands, THEN it clamps to max
- GIVEN rest, WHEN easing the toll, THEN it falls by the step without going below zero
- GIVEN a non positive rest step, WHEN resting, THEN the toll is unchanged
- GIVEN a neutral valor, WHEN reading the tolerance, THEN it is the base
- GIVEN a braver companion, WHEN reading the tolerance, THEN they bear more war
- GIVEN an extreme valor, WHEN reading the tolerance, THEN it stays within sane bounds
- GIVEN the toll reaches the tolerance, WHEN checking, THEN the companion wants out
- GIVEN the same toll, WHEN one is braver, THEN only the cautious one wants out
- GIVEN rising toll, WHEN banding a neutral companion, THEN it climbs fresh to spent
- GIVEN the same toll, WHEN valor differs, THEN the band shifts with tolerance
- GIVEN a landed companion, WHEN they retire, THEN they step back to govern
- GIVEN a landless companion, WHEN they retire, THEN they leave your service
- GIVEN each band, WHEN describing, THEN the reading reflects the band
- GIVEN the minimum service days, WHEN reading the constant, THEN it is two calradian years
- GIVEN service at exactly the minimum, WHEN checking tenure, THEN it has been served long enough
- GIVEN service one day short of the minimum, WHEN checking tenure, THEN it has not been served long enough
- GIVEN a brand new recruit, WHEN checking tenure, THEN it has not been served long enough

### WitnessDedupTests.cs

_Documents when two witness entries are the same person, so the "Present:" list never shows a duplicate (player report: bringing a companion who is already present listed their name twice)._

- GIVEN two entries with the same hero id, WHEN compared, THEN they are the same even if names differ
- GIVEN two entries with different hero ids, WHEN compared, THEN they are distinct
- GIVEN a hero and a same named heroless bystander, WHEN compared, THEN they stay distinct
- GIVEN two heroless entries with the same name, WHEN compared, THEN they are the same
- GIVEN two heroless entries with different names, WHEN compared, THEN they are distinct
- GIVEN a null entry, WHEN compared, THEN it is never the same

### WitnessPresencePolicyTests.cs

_This policy decides how many AMBIENT hero witnesses a gathered conversation actually keeps, once the resolver has already collected them (WitnessResolver.ResolveAutoWitnesses / ApplyWitnessDensity). Its answer feeds straight into the system prompt: every witness it keeps gets a voice, a portrait, and a slot in [WITNESS_REACTION] exchanges, so an over-generous keep means the same crowded room players complained about, and an over-eager thinning means a lord's own audience quietly loses its retinue for no reason the player can see. The mod is LIVE, so the single rule every test here ultimately serves is: Bustling, the shipped default, must change NOTHING for a player who never opens the menu. The rng is injected so the crowd stays unpredictable in play (backed by MBRandom in the engine) while remaining fully deterministic here._

- GIVEN Bustling, WHEN filtering a large room, THEN every witness is kept unchanged
- GIVEN Bustling, WHEN the rng returns just under one, THEN nothing is dropped
- GIVEN Sparse, WHEN every witness would pass the probability roll, THEN the room is still capped to one
- GIVEN Normal, WHEN every witness would pass the probability roll, THEN the room is capped to two
- GIVEN Normal, WHEN the rng returns just below the probability, THEN the witness is kept
- GIVEN Normal, WHEN the rng returns exactly the probability, THEN the witness is dropped
- GIVEN Sparse, WHEN the rng straddles its own cutoff, THEN below keeps and at or above drops
- GIVEN an empty eligible list, WHEN filtering at any density, THEN the result is empty
- GIVEN a null eligible list, WHEN filtering, THEN the result is an empty list not a crash
- GIVEN a room smaller than the cap, WHEN filtering, THEN the whole room survives
- GIVEN several witnesses survive the roll, WHEN capped, THEN their original order is preserved
- GIVEN a null rng, WHEN filtering, THEN it degrades to an empty room instead of throwing
- GIVEN the three density levels, WHEN their probability and cap are read, THEN they match the shipped mapping
- GIVEN the three levels, WHEN ordered, THEN each one up genuinely means a fuller room

### WitnessReactionGateTests.cs

_This gate decides which [WITNESS_REACTION] lines a model emitted may be VOICED, and the stake is larger than a stray bubble on screen: a kept line is committed to the transcript, so it is replayed into every prompt rebuilt afterwards. A wrongly-kept line therefore does not appear once, it joins the conversation's history and the model keeps that speaker talking. The rule lived inside ChatViewModel until now, which meant it had NO test at all (the mod is net472, this project is net10), and that is exactly how one of these faults shipped and reached players through the 15C witness path._

- GIVEN a line from someone in the room, WHEN deciding, THEN it is voiced
- GIVEN a line from someone who is not in the room, WHEN deciding, THEN it is dropped
- GIVEN a line attributed to the speaker themselves, WHEN deciding, THEN it is dropped
- GIVEN the same line this speaker just said, WHEN deciding, THEN it is dropped
- GIVEN a repeat written in different casing, WHEN deciding, THEN it is still caught
- GIVEN a different line from the same speaker, WHEN deciding, THEN it is voiced
- GIVEN a name carrying its prompt role, WHEN deciding, THEN it is matched and named canonically
- GIVEN a synthetic witness with no hero behind it, WHEN deciding, THEN it may still speak
- GIVEN an empty line, WHEN deciding, THEN it is dropped as blank
- GIVEN nobody present, WHEN deciding, THEN nothing may be voiced

### WitnessSpeechDetectorTests.cs

_Player report: a non-active witness gives a wordless gesture ("*smirk deepens, remains silent, fingers drumming*") and the active NPC's Sprint 15C auto-reply then treats it as being blown off, escalating against a silence the same model just invented. This classifier is what the 15C gate consults to decide whether a witness reaction actually SPOKE; a wrong answer here either lets a pure gesture keep driving the auto-reply loop (the bug persists) or silences a witness who genuinely spoke (a real reply vanishes)._

- GIVEN the reported bug string, WHEN checking, THEN it has no spoken words
- GIVEN a gesture only reaction, WHEN checking, THEN it has no spoken words
- GIVEN a quoted line, WHEN checking, THEN it has spoken words
- GIVEN a gesture plus a quoted line, WHEN checking, THEN it has spoken words
- GIVEN plain prose with no asterisks, WHEN checking, THEN it has spoken words
- GIVEN null or whitespace, WHEN checking, THEN it has no spoken words
- GIVEN multiple gesture spans and nothing else, WHEN checking, THEN it has no spoken words
- GIVEN a stray unmatched asterisk around real words, WHEN checking, THEN it has spoken words

### WorldEventPrunerTests.cs

_Keeps the persisted world-event log bounded: WorldEventStore serializes the whole list to a save-file byte[] on every save, so an unbounded log is a growth-without-limit risk to the save (the same class of failure the mod already guards against for the NPC profile store). Two lifetimes, on purpose: third-party rumours age out on a window plus a hard cap; the player's own deeds are the chronicle and only get a very high safety cap; externally-fed "kept" deeds (Extension Surface) get their own smaller cap so a flooding third-party mod cannot crowd out either the player's record or ordinary gossip._

- GIVEN a null list, WHEN pruned, THEN it does not throw
- GIVEN a player deed far older than the window, WHEN pruned, THEN it is kept
- GIVEN a third party rumour at the window edge, WHEN pruned, THEN it is kept
- GIVEN a third party rumour older than the window, WHEN pruned, THEN it is dropped
- GIVEN more third party rumours than the cap, WHEN pruned, THEN only the cap remains
- GIVEN an external deed far older than the rumour window, WHEN pruned, THEN it is kept
- GIVEN more external deeds than their cap, WHEN pruned, THEN only the cap remains and the oldest drop first
- GIVEN a flood of external deeds, WHEN pruned, THEN they do not count against the ordinary rumour cap

### WorldFactRelevancePolicyTests.cs

_Protects the query-relevant world-fact scoring: the player asks a lord about Clan X's war and gets a hallucinated answer because the real fact ranked too low on ambient AWARENESS to be injected; entity-id relevance must surface it. This is deliberately downstream of the knowledge gate (RumorAwareness / GetKnownEventsRated), it only ranks candidates ALREADY cleared to be known, it never widens who knows what._

- GIVEN the event names a hero the player also named, WHEN scored, THEN it is relevant
- GIVEN strong keyword overlap with no entity match, WHEN scored, THEN it is relevant
- GIVEN no id match and no keyword overlap, WHEN scored, THEN it is not relevant
- GIVEN the only shared tokens are short or stop words, WHEN scored, THEN they do not count
- GIVEN exactly one short keyword overlap, WHEN scored, THEN it falls just short of relevant
- GIVEN two short keyword overlaps, WHEN scored, THEN it lands on the threshold and is relevant
- GIVEN one long keyword overlap, WHEN scored, THEN it alone is relevant

## Grudges

### Grudges/AnsweredGrievanceTests.cs

_that a grievance the player has ANSWERED is remembered as answered, and that answering it is not mending it._

- GIVEN an answered grievance, WHEN the holder reads it, THEN they know the account and are told not to ask again
- GIVEN a vendetta, WHEN it is answered, THEN it is exactly as heavy and exactly as unforgivable
- GIVEN a mendable grievance, WHEN it is answered, THEN nothing about its weight changes
- GIVEN an answered grievance, WHEN asking whether it still drives a confrontation, THEN it does not
- GIVEN a resolved grievance, WHEN answered, THEN it is refused and it drives nothing
- GIVEN an empty account, WHEN recorded, THEN the grievance stays unanswered
- GIVEN a long multiline account, WHEN recorded, THEN it is kept as one bounded line
- GIVEN a grievance the player did not know of, WHEN answered, THEN it becomes known
- GIVEN a grudge saved before the field existed, WHEN loaded, THEN it reads as never answered
- GIVEN a live grievance, WHEN hardened, THEN it deepens and never past 100
- GIVEN a mendable grievance, WHEN hardened, THEN it stays mendable
- GIVEN a resolved grievance, WHEN hardened, THEN it stays resolved and unchanged

### Grudges/CaptorSceneGrudgePolicyTests.cs

_CaptorSceneGrudgePolicy decides whether a closed captor scene (the player holding an NPC prisoner) plants a CaptiveAbuse grudge. It exists because a player reported (Mimo, Standard mode) that keeping a captive SAFE and well treated still planted an abuse grudge: the old code formed the grudge from the opening INTENT alone. The rule under test makes the intent only a CEILING and lets the player's actual CONDUCT decide, with a conservative fallback when the conduct cannot be read. These tests pin both edges: a merciful scene must never resent the player, and a genuinely abusive one still must._

- GIVEN a reward scene, WHEN deciding, THEN no grudge regardless of conduct
- GIVEN an interrogation with no conduct read, WHEN deciding, THEN no grudge
- GIVEN a domination with no conduct read, WHEN deciding, THEN no grudge
- GIVEN torture with no conduct read, WHEN deciding, THEN it forms at full severity
- GIVEN a domination with confirmed abuse, WHEN deciding, THEN it forms at the intent ceiling
- GIVEN confirmed mercy, WHEN deciding, THEN nothing forms even for a torture intent
- GIVEN an interrogation with confirmed abuse, WHEN deciding, THEN it is capped at the intent

### Grudges/ExecutedKinRulesTests.cs

_Grudges pillar, Tier 3 (the vendetta end of the spectrum): one behaviour per test, Gabriel reads these as the design documentation for ExecutedKin formation. GrudgePolicy.RigidityAt already fixes this source's rigidity to VendettaRigidity regardless of the magnitude this rule proposes, so these tests only cover Forms/Magnitude/KnownToPlayer, never rigidity itself (that is GrudgePolicyTests's job)._

- GIVEN any close kin of an executed lord, WHEN evaluating, THEN a grudge always forms known to the player
- GIVEN a spouse or child, WHEN evaluating, THEN the magnitude is the heaviest tier
- GIVEN a parent or sibling, WHEN evaluating, THEN the magnitude is lighter than spouse or child but still a vendetta
- GIVEN kin who already resented the player, WHEN evaluating, THEN the magnitude is bumped
- GIVEN a bump that would exceed the 100 scale, WHEN evaluating, THEN the magnitude is clamped to 100
- GIVEN null facts, WHEN evaluating executed kin, THEN nothing forms
- GIVEN the lightest forming case, WHEN evaluating, THEN the magnitude clears the formation floor and the grudge is a true vendetta regardless of regard

### Grudges/GrudgeBackfillRulesTests.cs

_These rules turn markers ALREADY sitting in existing saves (a jealousy NotableEvent, a terminal InformalQuest status, the divorce set-aside Betrayal text, the bastard-claim Scandal WorldEvent) into backfilled Grudges, so players upgrading to the Grudges pillar do not silently lose every grievance that predates it. GrudgeMigration.SynthesizeGrudges consumes the MigrationCandidate each method here emits; a wrong Marker/HolderId/TargetId/EventDay fabricates a grudge that never happened or corrupts which NPC nurses it. The rule is deliberately CONSERVATIVE (the ROADMAP's hard save-migration requirement): every method recognizes ONLY its own exact marker and returns null on anything else, including a half-match, rather than guess._

- GIVEN a bastard DISCOVERY scandal not a claim, WHEN mapping, THEN it is conservatively skipped
- GIVEN a betrayal event that is NOT the divorce marker, WHEN mapping, THEN it is conservatively skipped
- GIVEN a jealousy ordinal event, WHEN mapping, THEN a jealousy candidate is produced
- GIVEN a non jealousy non betrayal event, WHEN mapping, THEN nothing is produced
- GIVEN a non scandal world event involving the player, WHEN mapping, THEN it is skipped
- GIVEN a profile event with no holder id, WHEN mapping, THEN it is conservatively skipped
- GIVEN a quest status other than expired or abandoned, WHEN mapping, THEN nothing is produced
- GIVEN a scandal event that does not involve the player, WHEN mapping, THEN it is skipped
- GIVEN a short summary, WHEN mapping, THEN it is carried verbatim
- GIVEN a summary longer than the cap, WHEN mapping, THEN the cause is truncated
- GIVEN an abandoned quest with no deadline, WHEN mapping, THEN it anchors on the issued day
- GIVEN an expired quest, WHEN mapping, THEN a failed quest candidate anchors on the deadline day
- GIVEN no actors, WHEN mapping a bastard claim, THEN it is skipped
- GIVEN only half the divorce marker text, WHEN mapping, THEN it is conservatively skipped
- GIVEN the exact bastard claim marker text, WHEN mapping, THEN a bastard claim candidate is produced
- GIVEN the exact divorce set aside marker text, WHEN mapping, THEN a divorce candidate is produced

### Grudges/GrudgeMigrationTests.cs

_SAVE-MIGRATION HARD REQUIREMENT (ROADMAP 2026-07-03): GrudgeMigration.SynthesizeGrudges is the one-shot backfill GrudgeBehavior.RunBackfillIfNeeded runs on EVERY session launch, translating markers already sitting in an OLD save (jealousy, scheme_warned/abetted, failed_quest, divorce, bastard_claim/blackmail) into first-class, resolvable Grudge records. Additive-only, conservative (unknown markers skipped, never guessed), idempotent (dedup by a RECOMPUTED holder+source+day key, never the stored Id, since a live-planted grudge carries a random Guid instead), and versioned (a no-op once the ledger is stamped at or beyond MigrationVersion). Get this wrong and a player loading an old save either loses their grudge history outright, or gets it replanted, and double-counted, on every relaunch._

- GIVEN a candidate below the formation floor, WHEN migrating, THEN it is skipped by the same anti noise guardrail
- GIVEN a candidate marked unattributable, WHEN migrating, THEN it is skipped however large
- GIVEN a grudge already covering the same event but carrying a NON canonical id, WHEN migrating, THEN it is still recognized as a duplicate
- GIVEN a ledger already stamped at the current version, WHEN migrating again, THEN it is a pure no op
- GIVEN a ledger seeded from a prior pass, WHEN migrating the same candidates again, THEN no new grudges are produced
- GIVEN a ledger stamped beyond the current version, WHEN migrating, THEN it is still a no op
- GIVEN a migration pass, WHEN it runs, THEN it never mutates the existing grudges collection it was handed
- GIVEN a save carrying every tier one marker, WHEN migrating for the first time, THEN a grudge is synthesized for each
- GIVEN a synthesized grudge, WHEN inspecting it, THEN it carries the original cause day and an unresolved state
- GIVEN a synthesized grudge, WHEN inspecting its shape, THEN it exposes no relation delta to replay or refund
- GIVEN a tier two marker like delivered prisoner, WHEN migrating, THEN it is also skipped
- GIVEN an empty candidate list, WHEN migrating, THEN nothing is produced
- GIVEN an unrecognized marker, WHEN migrating, THEN it is skipped not guessed at
- GIVEN no candidates at all, WHEN migrating, THEN the result is empty but not a no op
- GIVEN one candidate already present in the ledger, WHEN migrating a mixed batch, THEN only the new one is added
- GIVEN the same candidates and no ledger, WHEN synthesizing twice independently, THEN the two results are deeply identical
- GIVEN a candidate already decayed to zero by today, WHEN today is supplied, THEN it is skipped

### Grudges/GrudgeNarratorTests.cs

_GrudgeNarrator turns a Grudge's raw numbers (Magnitude, Rigidity) into the two pieces of prose the mod actually ships: the encyclopedia's "Grievances" sentence and the one-liner injected straight into the NPC's LLM prompt (EncounterContextBuilder.ResolveGrudgeNote). The prompt line is not flavour text, it steers how the model plays the NPC this turn: wrong wording lets a HIDDEN grudge's true cause slip (spoiling the late-unmasking beat the concealment design exists for), a stray digit breaks the numberless-narrative rule the ROADMAP ratifies for both surfaces, or a wrong resolution hint sends the player chasing an action the grudge does not actually accept. These tests pin the exact wording and gating so a wording tweak cannot silently reintroduce one of those._

- GIVEN a cause that ends in a period, WHEN building the display line, THEN the sentence ends in a single period
- GIVEN a fully decayed grudge, WHEN building the display line, THEN nothing is surfaced
- GIVEN a fully decayed grudge, WHEN building the prompt line, THEN nothing is surfaced
- GIVEN a hidden grudge, WHEN building the prompt line, THEN the npc is instructed to conceal it
- GIVEN a high rigidity, WHEN naming the band, THEN it reads as a deep grudge
- GIVEN a known grudge, WHEN building the prompt line, THEN the npc may name it openly
- GIVEN a low rigidity, WHEN naming the band, THEN it reads as a fresh wound
- GIVEN a mid rigidity, WHEN naming the band, THEN it reads as an old bitterness
- GIVEN a missing target name, WHEN building the display line, THEN it falls back to them rather than blank
- GIVEN a null grudge, WHEN building the display line, THEN it returns null not throw
- GIVEN a null grudge, WHEN building the prompt line, THEN it returns null not throw
- GIVEN a resolved grudge, WHEN building the display line, THEN nothing is surfaced
- GIVEN a resolved grudges zero effective magnitude, WHEN building the prompt line, THEN nothing is surfaced
- GIVEN a vendetta grudge with the hint discovered, WHEN building the display line, THEN it reads as unforgivable with no mending path
- GIVEN a very low rigidity, WHEN naming the band, THEN it reads as a passing grievance
- GIVEN every rigidity band, WHEN building the display line, THEN no digits ever appear in the generated text
- GIVEN every rigidity band, WHEN building the prompt line, THEN no digits ever appear in the generated text
- GIVEN maximum rigidity, WHEN naming the band, THEN it reads as unforgivable
- GIVEN no cause summary, WHEN building the display line, THEN a fallback phrase is used instead of blank text
- GIVEN no cause summary, WHEN building the prompt line, THEN a fallback phrase is used instead of blank text
- GIVEN the grudge targets a named npc, WHEN building the display line, THEN the npc is named by name
- GIVEN the grudge targets the player, WHEN building the display line, THEN the player is named you
- GIVEN the resolution hint is discovered, WHEN building the display line, THEN the hint matches the resolution kind
- GIVEN the resolution hint is not discovered, WHEN building the display line, THEN no hint sentence is appended

### Grudges/GrudgePolicyTests.cs

_GrudgePolicy is the arbiter behind every NPC grievance in the mod: whether an event is worth grudging over at all (formation), how hard it will be to mend (rigidity, seeded from the holder's regard for the target), how it fades with time (decay), which grudges are allowed to surface at once per NPC (the cap), and what kind of gesture actually mends one (resolution). GrudgeBehavior plants through ShouldForm/TryAdmit, EncounterContextBuilder/ChatViewModel colour the prompt through SelectDominant, and GrudgeResolutionService routes Amends/Gift/Deed through ResolutionKindFor/Resolve._

- GIVEN a delivered prisoner grudge, WHEN mapping the resolution kind, THEN it wants a real deed
- GIVEN a fief revoked grudge, WHEN mapping the resolution kind, THEN it wants a real deed regardless of rigidity
- GIVEN an expelled grudge, WHEN mapping the resolution kind, THEN it wants a real deed regardless of rigidity
- GIVEN a broken oath grudge, WHEN soft, THEN it wants amends
- GIVEN a broken oath grudge, WHEN hardened, THEN it wants a real deed
- GIVEN a pact broken grudge, WHEN soft, THEN it wants amends
- GIVEN a pact broken grudge, WHEN hardened, THEN it wants a real deed
- GIVEN a full house but some entries are resolved or fully decayed, WHEN admitting, THEN only the live ones count toward the cap
- GIVEN a full house, WHEN the newcomer is no stronger than the weakest live grudge, THEN it is refused
- GIVEN a full house, WHEN the newcomer is strictly stronger than the weakest live grudge, THEN it is admitted and the weakest is named for eviction
- GIVEN a fully decayed grudge, WHEN selecting dominant, THEN it never surfaces
- GIVEN a hard jealousy grudge, WHEN mapping the resolution kind, THEN only time heals it
- GIVEN a null collection, WHEN selecting dominant, THEN it returns empty not null
- GIVEN a null live list, WHEN admitting a newcomer, THEN it is admitted
- GIVEN a possessive holder, WHEN a jealousy candidate forms, THEN the magnitude is amplified
- GIVEN a possessive holder, WHEN a non jealousy candidate forms, THEN the amplifier does not apply
- GIVEN a resolvable grudge, WHEN resolving it, THEN it is marked resolved on that day
- GIVEN a resolved grudge, WHEN reading effective magnitude, THEN it reads as zero
- GIVEN a resolved grudge, WHEN selecting dominant, THEN it never surfaces
- GIVEN a soft divorce grudge, WHEN mapping the resolution kind, THEN a gift suffices
- GIVEN a soft grudge, WHEN checking resolvability, THEN it is resolvable
- GIVEN a vendetta, WHEN attempting to resolve it, THEN it refuses and leaves it untouched
- GIVEN a vendetta, WHEN checking resolvability, THEN it is unresolvable
- GIVEN a vendetta, WHEN reading effective magnitude, THEN it never decays
- GIVEN an already resolved grudge, WHEN checking resolvability, THEN it is not resolvable again
- GIVEN an unattributable event, WHEN deciding formation, THEN it refuses however large
- GIVEN any source that reaches maximum rigidity, WHEN mapping the resolution kind, THEN it is unforgivable
- GIVEN bastard blackmail, WHEN mapping the resolution kind, THEN it is always transactional regardless of rigidity
- GIVEN enough elapsed time, WHEN reading effective magnitude, THEN it floors at zero never negative
- GIVEN extreme negative regard and high calculating, WHEN computing rigidity, THEN it stops one short of a vendetta
- GIVEN a grudge formed at the worst possible regard, WHEN judging it, THEN it can still be mended
- GIVEN an executed kin grudge, WHEN computing rigidity, THEN it is still a full vendetta
- GIVEN a grudge frozen by the old saturation, WHEN healing, THEN it becomes mendable again
- GIVEN a true vendetta, WHEN healing, THEN it keeps its full rigidity
- GIVEN an already healed or ordinary grudge, WHEN healing again, THEN nothing moves
- GIVEN extreme positive regard and high mercy, WHEN computing rigidity, THEN it clamps at zero not negative
- GIVEN fewer live grudges than the cap, WHEN admitting a newcomer, THEN it is always admitted with nothing to evict
- GIVEN high calculating, WHEN computing rigidity, THEN it pushes rigidity up
- GIVEN high calculating, WHEN deciding formation, THEN the bar is lowered and a lesser slight still forms
- GIVEN high calculating, WHEN reading effective magnitude, THEN it never decays
- GIVEN high mercy, WHEN computing rigidity, THEN it pulls rigidity down
- GIVEN high mercy, WHEN deciding formation, THEN the bar is raised and a middling slight no longer forms
- GIVEN high mercy, WHEN reading effective magnitude, THEN it decays faster than a plain holder
- GIVEN high regard for the target, WHEN computing rigidity, THEN it is soft
- GIVEN magnitude at the floor, WHEN deciding formation, THEN it forms
- GIVEN magnitude below the floor, WHEN deciding formation, THEN it refuses
- GIVEN more live grudges than the cap, WHEN selecting dominant, THEN only the top ones surface
- GIVEN negative regard for the target, WHEN computing rigidity, THEN it is hard
- GIVEN null facts, WHEN deciding formation, THEN it refuses
- GIVEN the executed kin source at maximum rigidity, WHEN mapping the resolution kind, THEN it is unforgivable
- GIVEN the executed kin source, WHEN computing rigidity, THEN it is always maximum regardless of regard
- GIVEN the possessive amplifier, WHEN it pushes a borderline jealousy event over the floor, THEN it forms
- GIVEN time passing, WHEN reading effective magnitude, THEN it softens

### Grudges/GrudgeResolutionPolicyTests.cs

_Grudges pillar, resolution half: decides whether a player's offered gesture (Amends, Gift or Deed) toward a held grudge Resolves it, Softens it, or is Rejected. The verdict feeds GrudgeResolutionService.TryResolveDominant, which mutates the live Grudge, bumps the holder's personal regard, and records a chronicle "made peace" line._

- GIVEN a calculating holder, WHEN evaluating a matching gift at the raised bar, THEN it still resolves
- GIVEN a calculating holder, WHEN evaluating a non matching gesture, THEN it is rejected outright
- GIVEN a calculating holder, WHEN evaluating a time kind grudge, THEN even a generous gesture does not nudge it
- GIVEN a gift covering nearly the whole bar, WHEN evaluating, THEN the softening is still capped short of resolving
- GIVEN a grudge already fully decayed, WHEN evaluating a gesture, THEN there is nothing left to resolve
- GIVEN a low mercy holder, WHEN evaluating a non matching gesture, THEN it is rejected outright
- GIVEN a matching gesture below the resolve cutoff, WHEN evaluating, THEN it resolves in one gesture
- GIVEN a matching gift at or above the bar, WHEN evaluating, THEN it resolves the grudge
- GIVEN a matching gift below the bar, WHEN evaluating, THEN it softens proportional to how much of the bar it covered
- GIVEN a non matching but positive gesture, WHEN evaluating, THEN it only softens never resolves
- GIVEN a non matching gesture, WHEN compared to a matching one, THEN it softens less
- GIVEN a null attempt, WHEN evaluating, THEN it rejects
- GIVEN a resolved outcome, WHEN evaluating, THEN the reduction always equals the full effective magnitude
- GIVEN a time kind grudge, WHEN evaluating a deed, THEN it does not nudge it at all
- GIVEN a time kind grudge, WHEN evaluating a gift, THEN it is never resolved but is nudged slightly
- GIVEN a time kind grudge, WHEN evaluating amends, THEN it is also nudged
- GIVEN a vendetta rigidity, WHEN evaluating any gesture, THEN it is always rejected
- GIVEN a very rigid matching gesture, WHEN evaluating, THEN it softens rather than resolves so it takes several
- GIVEN a zero value gift, WHEN evaluating, THEN it is rejected
- GIVEN an extreme mercy value, WHEN computing the gift bar, THEN the price multiplier never drops below the tuned floor
- GIVEN an unforgivable resolution kind, WHEN evaluating a gesture, THEN it is rejected even at low rigidity
- GIVEN any softened outcome, WHEN evaluating, THEN the reduction never exceeds the effective magnitude
- GIVEN high calculating, WHEN computing the gift bar, THEN it is higher than a plain holders
- GIVEN high honor, WHEN evaluating a matching amends gesture, THEN it resolves a grudge a plain holder would only soften
- GIVEN high honor, WHEN evaluating a matching deed gesture, THEN the bonus does not apply
- GIVEN high mercy, WHEN computing the gift bar, THEN it is lower than a plain holders
- GIVEN high mercy, WHEN evaluating the same below bar gift, THEN it softens more than a plain holders

### Grudges/GrudgeToneArbiterTests.cs

_Grudges pillar, tone arbiter: a strong grudge must outrank stated warmth. These tests pin the three bands, their boundaries, the known-vs-hidden wording split at GrudgeDominates, and (11/07/2026) how a warm personal regard raises both bars so a genuine bond needs a heavier grudge before it is tempered or dominated (fixes the "spouse fixates on the divorce grudge despite +31 regard" bug)._

- GIVEN a magnitude below the temper threshold, WHEN resolving the ruling, THEN warmth reads unshadowed
- GIVEN a magnitude exactly at the dominance threshold, WHEN resolving the ruling, THEN the grudge dominates
- GIVEN a magnitude exactly at the temper threshold, WHEN resolving the ruling, THEN warmth is tempered
- GIVEN a magnitude just below the dominance threshold, WHEN resolving the ruling, THEN warmth is still tempered not dominated
- GIVEN a magnitude well above the dominance threshold, WHEN resolving the ruling, THEN the grudge dominates
- GIVEN a reputation value, WHEN computing regard tempering, THEN it matches the expected point rise
- GIVEN cold negative regard, WHEN resolving a magnitude 40 grudge, THEN the grudge still dominates
- GIVEN deep trust at regard 50, WHEN resolving a magnitude 40 grudge, THEN warmth reads unshadowed
- GIVEN every line, WHEN checking style, THEN no em dashes appear
- GIVEN every ruling and knownness combination, WHEN asking for the line, THEN no digits ever appear
- GIVEN neutral regard, WHEN resolving the ruling, THEN just below the temper band reads unshadowed
- GIVEN neutral regard, WHEN resolving the ruling, THEN the dominance band matches todays behaviour
- GIVEN neutral regard, WHEN resolving the ruling, THEN the temper band matches todays behaviour
- GIVEN no grudge magnitude, WHEN resolving the ruling, THEN warmth reads unshadowed
- GIVEN reputation at or below the warmth tempering floor, WHEN resolving the ruling, THEN only the magnitude band decides it
- GIVEN the grudge dominates and is hidden from the player, WHEN asking for the line, THEN it reads as a concealment ruling
- GIVEN the grudge dominates and is known to the player, WHEN asking for the line, THEN it reads as open coolness
- GIVEN the grudge dominates, WHEN knownness differs, THEN the known and hidden lines differ
- GIVEN the reported spouse regard of 31, WHEN resolving a magnitude 30 grudge, THEN warmth reads unshadowed
- GIVEN the reported spouse regard of 31, WHEN resolving a magnitude 31 grudge, THEN warmth is tempered
- GIVEN the reported spouse regard of 31, WHEN resolving a magnitude 40 divorce grudge, THEN warmth is tempered not dominated
- GIVEN warmth tempered, WHEN asking for the line, THEN it reads as a reserve showing through
- GIVEN warmth tempered, WHEN knownness differs, THEN the line is identical either way
- GIVEN warmth unshadowed, WHEN asking for the line, THEN there is none

### Grudges/Tier2GrudgeRulesTests.cs

_Grudges pillar, Tier 2 source catalog: one behaviour per test, Gabriel reads these as the design documentation for DeliveredPrisoner / CaptiveAbuse / SwayExposed / UnblessedMarriage formation._

- GIVEN a captive delivered to their own enemy, WHEN evaluating, THEN a grudge forms known to the player
- GIVEN a captive delivered to a non enemy, WHEN evaluating, THEN no grudge forms
- GIVEN a captive who already disliked the player, WHEN sold to their enemy, THEN the magnitude is bumped
- GIVEN null facts, WHEN evaluating delivered prisoner, THEN nothing forms
- GIVEN a benign captive scene severity zero, WHEN evaluating, THEN nothing forms
- GIVEN a captive scene at or above severity one, WHEN evaluating, THEN a grudge forms
- GIVEN rising severity, WHEN evaluating, THEN magnitude climbs steeply
- GIVEN the player as direct perpetrator, WHEN evaluating, THEN the magnitude is heaviest at any given severity
- GIVEN grievous severity with the player as perpetrator, WHEN evaluating, THEN the magnitude peaks but is not a forced vendetta
- GIVEN null facts, WHEN evaluating captive abuse, THEN nothing forms
- GIVEN the rumour never reached the target, WHEN evaluating, THEN nothing forms
- GIVEN the rumour reached the target, WHEN evaluating, THEN a grudge forms hidden from the player
- GIVEN a harder sway, WHEN evaluating, THEN the grudge magnitude tracks the sway strength
- GIVEN null facts, WHEN evaluating sway exposed, THEN nothing forms
- GIVEN an unblessed marriage still standing, WHEN evaluating, THEN a grudge forms known to the player
- GIVEN a blessed marriage, WHEN evaluating, THEN nothing forms even if still married
- GIVEN an unblessed marriage that has ended, WHEN evaluating, THEN nothing forms because this grudge is meant to clear on divorce
- GIVEN null facts, WHEN evaluating unblessed marriage, THEN nothing forms
- GIVEN every tier 2 rules lightest forming case, WHEN evaluating, THEN the magnitude clears the formation floor

## Guardrails

### Guardrails/EngineRegistryGuardTests.cs

_Focused unit tests for the pure decision core of the engine-registry initialization guardrail (EngineRegistryGuard). The whole-assembly IL scan in EngineRegistryInitializationGuardTests only reports the truth if this classifier is right, so these pin its two edges directly: a blacklisted registry among safe references IS flagged, and engine-agnostic references are NOT. See EngineRegistryGuard's header for the full 2026-08-05 CouncilWardBehavior / DefaultSkills save-load crash this whole guardrail exists to prevent._

- GIVEN a constructor referencing DefaultSkills amid safe types, WHEN classified, THEN DefaultSkills is flagged
- GIVEN a constructor referencing only engine agnostic types, WHEN classified, THEN nothing is flagged
- GIVEN a process global or registry type name, WHEN asked, THEN it is unsafe
- GIVEN a non blacklisted or empty name, WHEN asked, THEN it is safe

### Guardrails/EngineRegistryInitializationGuardTests.cs

_A WHITEBOX guardrail: it inspects the COMPILED mod assembly (CalradiaRemembers.dll) with Mono.Cecil, reading IL from the file WITHOUT loading or executing it (so the TaleWorlds DLLs never enter this test process), and FAILS the build if any constructor reintroduces the load-order landmine that crashed the game on 2026-08-05._

- GIVEN the compiled mod assembly, WHEN scanning every constructor, THEN none touches an unsafe engine registry

## Interception

### Interception/AudienceTopicLineTests.cs

_that the player is told what an NPC wants, before deciding whether to hear it._

- GIVEN a matter to raise, WHEN the invitation is written, THEN it names the subject
- GIVEN no subject, WHEN the invitation is written, THEN it promises nothing
- GIVEN a summary longer than the box, WHEN written, THEN it is cut and marked
- GIVEN a summary that ends in a full stop, WHEN written, THEN the clause does not double it
- GIVEN a summary across several lines, WHEN written, THEN it reads as one clause

### Interception/RecentMatterSelectorTests.cs

_RecentMatterSelector decides the ONE thing a lord raises when he approaches the player about a recent matter (design [[project_npc_recent_matter]], phase 1): the most recent one he knows of, within a recency window, that he has not already raised. If this picked the wrong one, a lord who just fought beside the player would still dredge up a stale distant rumor (fkasad's report) instead of the fresh shared battle; if it forgot the once-per-NPC guard, he would raise the same matter every time they met. These pin that selection._

- GIVEN a fresh lived memory and an older rumor, WHEN picking, THEN the fresher lived memory wins
- GIVEN a fresher rumor than the lived memory, WHEN picking, THEN the fresher rumor wins
- GIVEN the freshest matter already raised, WHEN picking, THEN it falls through to the next
- GIVEN only stale or raised matters, WHEN picking, THEN nothing is returned
- GIVEN a future dated or blank key matter, WHEN picking, THEN it is ignored
- GIVEN two matters tied on day and register, WHEN picking, THEN the result is deterministic
- GIVEN a fresher world gossip and an older personal matter, WHEN picking, THEN the personal matter wins
- GIVEN only world gossip left, WHEN picking, THEN the gossip is raised
- GIVEN no candidates, WHEN picking, THEN nothing is returned
- GIVEN his own village burning and a fresher distant war, WHEN picking, THEN he raises the village
- GIVEN two matters that both touch him, WHEN picking, THEN the greater one wins
- GIVEN two matters alike in every other way, WHEN picking, THEN the fresher one wins
- GIVEN a personal memory of no size and a great public event, WHEN picking, THEN the personal one wins
- GIVEN two personal matters, WHEN picking, THEN the fresher one still wins
- GIVEN only a distant matter, WHEN picking, THEN it is still raised

## Items

### Items/GiftNameResolverTests.cs

_Player report (Windmolen): married, pregnant wife, offered her "a pendant" in free text. The LLM emitted give_item item: pendant, and the old exact-name-only match failed even though the player held a "Jewelry" trade good: the bridge answered "(You don't seem to have 'pendant' to give.)" and the wife "was not amused." GiftNameResolver.Resolve is the fix under test here: a narrated personal-gift word should resolve to a real matching roster item (by exact name, by a shared word, or by a jewelry synonym mapped to category) instead of hard-failing, and when several items could match, it must always prefer the CHEAPEST one so a narrated flourish never accidentally siphons the player's most valuable possession._

- GIVEN pendant requested and only a jewelry trade good held, WHEN resolving, THEN it resolves to the jewelry item
- GIVEN the exact item name requested, WHEN resolving, THEN tier a wins over any synonym route
- GIVEN a word shared with a candidates full name, WHEN resolving, THEN the token tier resolves it
- GIVEN two jewelry candidates of different value, WHEN a synonym is requested, THEN the cheaper one is chosen
- GIVEN two candidates sharing a token, WHEN resolving, THEN the cheaper one is chosen
- GIVEN a trivially short requested word, WHEN resolving, THEN it never spuriously matches
- GIVEN no plausible match in the roster, WHEN resolving, THEN it returns none
- GIVEN a blank request or an empty or null candidate list, WHEN resolving, THEN it is safe and returns none

## Jealousy

### Jealousy/AcceptedPluralJealousyTests.cs

_what it costs a partner to have SAID she accepts the player's other wives, when her own people's custom says it is betrayal._

- GIVEN a partner who accepted against her culture, WHEN the player takes another, THEN it wounds her less than a betrayal
- GIVEN the three tracks, WHEN compared at the same act, THEN accepting against your culture sits between them
- GIVEN years of it, WHEN her regard has fallen to wary, THEN it stops costing regard and costs only the scene
- GIVEN one more slight from just above the floor, WHEN it lands, THEN it takes her to the floor and no further
- GIVEN a warm partner, WHEN the player marries another, THEN it still costs her the full softened wound
- GIVEN a positive delta, WHEN the floor is asked, THEN it is returned untouched
- GIVEN a partner whose people share partners, WHEN the track is chosen, THEN it is competition whether or not she ever agreed
- GIVEN an exclusive culture and a partner who said yes, WHEN the track is chosen, THEN it is the softened one
- GIVEN an exclusive culture and a partner who never agreed, WHEN the track is chosen, THEN it is betrayal
- GIVEN a liaison outside what she agreed to, WHEN the track is chosen, THEN it is betrayal at full price
- GIVEN a sharing culture but a liaison kept from her, WHEN the track is chosen, THEN it is still betrayal
- GIVEN an acceptance she has since withdrawn, WHEN the track is chosen, THEN she is back on her cultures track

### Jealousy/PluralAcceptancePolicyTests.cs

_who can be asked to accept a plural household, and who cannot be asked at all._

- GIVEN a devoted wife of an exclusive people, WHEN the question arises, THEN she may say she accepts
- GIVEN a wife of an exclusive people who is merely fond, WHEN the question arises, THEN she cannot be asked
- GIVEN a wife whose people already share partners, WHEN the question arises, THEN no devotion is required
- GIVEN a woman who is not a committed partner, WHEN the question arises, THEN there is nothing for her to accept
- GIVEN a partner who has already said it, WHEN the question arises, THEN she is not asked again
- GIVEN a couple who already hold open terms, WHEN the question arises, THEN the narrower one is not offered
- GIVEN the bar for accepting against your own culture, WHEN read, THEN it is devotion itself

- GIVEN the source as it stands, WHEN the modder api is regenerated, THEN it matches the committed map

- GIVEN the approval script, WHEN read, THEN its filter still selects these fixtures
- GIVEN the older rules script, WHEN read, THEN it delegates rather than writing the file itself

## Narrative

### Narrative/NarrativePatchComposerTests.cs

_that another module can add to the prompt's background prose without destroying anything._

- GIVEN a patch that only adds, WHEN composed, THEN the base text survives whole
- GIVEN two modules contributing, WHEN composed, THEN both contributions are present
- GIVEN a later module removing an earlier one line, WHEN composed, THEN the later one wins
- GIVEN a patch that removes a base line, WHEN composed, THEN that line is gone
- GIVEN a patch that replaces, WHEN composed, THEN nothing of the base remains
- GIVEN a contribution written as a bulleted list, WHEN composed, THEN nothing is deleted
- GIVEN an empty remove directive, WHEN composed, THEN it is ignored rather than emptying everything
- GIVEN a removal from the middle, WHEN composed, THEN no run of blank lines is left behind
- GIVEN no patches at all, WHEN composed, THEN the text is what the mod shipped

## Prisoners

### Prisoners/PrisonerCustodyPolicyTests.cs

_PrisonerCustodyPolicy decides which prisons are the player's and merges the captives they hold, deduped. It backs BuyPrisonerVerb.PlayerHeldHeroPrisoners, which gates three surfaces at once: the in-conversation buy_prisoner offer, the ransom-letter trigger, and that letter's Accept button. Player report: a ransom offer arrived with only a Close button for a lord who had NOT escaped (still behind bars in the player's own city), because the old enumeration saw only the travelling party. These tests pin the fix: a settlement the player's clan owns counts as the player's custody, a prison it does not own never does, and a captive is counted once._

- GIVEN a settlement owned by the players clan, WHEN checked, THEN it is the players custody
- GIVEN the main party, WHEN checked, THEN it is the players custody
- GIVEN a prison that is neither the main party nor an owned settlement, WHEN checked, THEN it is not custody
- GIVEN captives in the party and an owned town, WHEN merged, THEN both are held
- GIVEN a captive in a foreign settlement, WHEN merged, THEN it is excluded
- GIVEN the same captive id in two owned prisons, WHEN merged, THEN it appears once in first seen order
- GIVEN blank and null ids, WHEN merged, THEN they are dropped
- GIVEN null or empty input, WHEN merged, THEN it is empty and does not throw

## Rescue

### Rescue/CaptorGrudgePolicyTests.cs

_Rescue pillar: when the player forcibly frees a held prisoner, this policy decides whether the CAPTOR who lost that prize ever traces the deed back to the player, how hard the resulting grudge bites, and whether the player is told about it. RescueBehavior consumes all four members directly to gate its GrudgeSource.PrisonerRescued plant. All four constants are still TUNE (first-pass, not yet play-tested, per the production header): these tests pin the CURRENT numbers and their comparison directions so a retune is a deliberate, visible change here, not a silent drift. Get the public/private split wrong and a quiet field release either always or never gets traced, undermining the "public is easier to trace" design the whole policy exists to express._

- GIVEN a public action, WHEN reading discovery chance, THEN it is the public constant
- GIVEN a private action, WHEN reading discovery chance, THEN it is the private constant
- GIVEN a public action and a roll just below the public threshold, WHEN checking discovery, THEN it is discovered
- GIVEN a public action and a roll at the public threshold, WHEN checking discovery, THEN it is not discovered
- GIVEN a private action and a roll just below the private threshold, WHEN checking discovery, THEN it is discovered
- GIVEN a private action and a roll at the private threshold, WHEN checking discovery, THEN it is not discovered
- GIVEN a public action, WHEN reading grudge magnitude, THEN it is thirty
- GIVEN a private action, WHEN reading grudge magnitude, THEN it is twenty
- GIVEN a public action, WHEN checking if known to player, THEN it is true
- GIVEN a private action, WHEN checking if known to player, THEN it is false

### Rescue/RescueEligibilityPolicyTests.cs

_Rescue awareness (v1.30.5, ratified 2026-07-11): freeing a captive lord by force now earns real, LASTING consequences (gratitude + a memory for the freed lord and family, friends warm, the captor may grudge it)._

- GIVEN a lord freed after a battle the player won, WHEN evaluated, THEN it counts as a rescue
- GIVEN the player did not win the battle, WHEN evaluated, THEN it is not a rescue
- GIVEN the prisoner was not released after the battle, WHEN evaluated, THEN it is not a rescue
- GIVEN the freed prisoner is not a lord, WHEN evaluated, THEN it is not a rescue
- GIVEN the freed prisoner is a bandit, WHEN evaluated, THEN it is not a rescue
- GIVEN the freed prisoner is in the players own clan, WHEN evaluated, THEN it is not a rescue
- GIVEN the freed prisoner is the player, WHEN evaluated, THEN it is not a rescue
- GIVEN the player chose to free them after winning, WHEN weighing the release, THEN it can be a rescue
- GIVEN the engine freed them after the battle, WHEN weighing the release, THEN it can be a rescue
- GIVEN any other ending, WHEN weighing the release, THEN it is never a rescue

### Rescue/RescueGratitudePolicyTests.cs

_Rescue awareness (v1.30.5): once RescueEligibilityPolicy has confirmed a rescue is real, this policy decides how each person in the freed lord's circle reacts. RescueBehavior.ApplyGratitude reads Magnitude to move regard and RecordMemory to decide whether a lasting first-person memory is written; ResolveFriends reads QualifiesAsFriend to build the (capped) friend list. Self and family both remember the debt; a mere friend only warms a little and keeps no memory, so getting either the magnitude or the recording flag wrong either invents a memory for someone who was not really involved, or forgets one the story earned._

- GIVEN the rescued prisoner themselves, WHEN resolving gratitude, THEN it is the self magnitude and recorded
- GIVEN a close family member, WHEN resolving gratitude, THEN it is the family magnitude and recorded
- GIVEN a friend, WHEN resolving gratitude, THEN it is the friend magnitude and not recorded
- GIVEN a relation one below the friend threshold, WHEN checking friendship, THEN it does not qualify
- GIVEN a relation at the friend threshold, WHEN checking friendship, THEN it qualifies
- GIVEN a child of the rescued prisoner, WHEN the debt is weighed, THEN they cannot feel it
- GIVEN an adult relative, WHEN the debt is weighed, THEN they feel it as before

## Social

### Social/PresenceDescriberTests.cs

_Documents how a presence tier becomes the NPC's "awareness of being" at the player's side, and the two thresholds it governs. The flagship case is the reported one: a crew member riding with the player between two ports should sound like a daily companion, never like someone abandoned for a season, so the co-travel tiers get a warm present-tense line AND suppress the elapsed-gap note entirely._

- GIVEN a companion riding in the party on land, WHEN describing, THEN a side by side land line and no gap
- GIVEN a companion in the party at sea, WHEN describing, THEN the bond is phrased in sea terms
- GIVEN a party marching in the players army on land, WHEN describing, THEN an army line and no gap
- GIVEN a party in the players army at sea, WHEN describing, THEN a fleet line
- GIVEN a shared settlement, WHEN describing, THEN a here now line naming the place and a gap is allowed
- GIVEN a shared settlement with no place name, WHEN describing, THEN a generic here now line
- GIVEN an npc away, WHEN describing, THEN no spatial line but the gap note is allowed

## Styles

### Styles/StyleCachePolicyTests.cs

_StyleCachePolicy decides whether the mod's StyleService must re-read a style .txt file from disk before building the next prompt, or may keep serving its cached text. Reading a file is expensive, so this is consulted before every message; the engine hands it what is cached and the file's cheap last-write stamp. Get it wrong and either the player picks a new style (or a modder edits the .txt mid-session) and the stale cached voice keeps applying, silently ignoring the change, or the engine pays a disk read on every single message even when nothing changed._

- GIVEN nothing cached yet, WHEN deciding, THEN it reloads
- GIVEN the same name and the same timestamp, WHEN deciding, THEN it does not reload
- GIVEN a different requested name, WHEN deciding, THEN it reloads
- GIVEN the same name, WHEN the timestamp changed, THEN it reloads
- GIVEN the same name, WHEN the timestamp is older than cached, THEN it still reloads

### Styles/StyleCatalogPolicyTests.cs

_StyleCatalogPolicy decides which narrative style NAME actually applies, given what the player asked for (from the chat-window popup) and the style .txt files that exist under ModuleData/Styles. Consumed by the mod's StyleService, which picks the active style file with this policy and then decides (via StyleCachePolicy) whether to re-read it before building the prompt. Get the name resolution wrong and either a deleted/renamed style file silently blanks the narrative voice instead of degrading to a known default, or the engine opens the wrong file because the popup showed a casing that does not match disk._

- GIVEN no style files available, WHEN resolving, THEN the default name is returned
- GIVEN a requested name matching a file case insensitively, WHEN resolving, THEN the files canonical spelling is returned
- GIVEN an unknown requested name, WHEN a default file exists, THEN its canonical spelling is returned
- GIVEN an unknown requested name, WHEN no default file exists, THEN the default style name constant is returned
- GIVEN a null or whitespace request, WHEN resolving, THEN it behaves like an unknown request
- GIVEN the readme file, WHEN checking selectability, THEN it is not selectable
- GIVEN a leading underscore, WHEN checking selectability, THEN it is not selectable
- GIVEN an ordinary style name, WHEN checking selectability, THEN it is selectable
- GIVEN a null or whitespace name, WHEN checking selectability, THEN it is not selectable
- GIVEN a custom name matching a shipped name, WHEN merging, THEN only one entry is returned
- GIVEN disjoint shipped and custom names, WHEN merging, THEN the union is returned
- GIVEN a custom name differing only in case, WHEN merging, THEN it still dedupes to one entry
- GIVEN no custom names, WHEN merging, THEN the shipped names are returned unchanged
- GIVEN mixed shipped and custom names, WHEN merging, THEN the result is sorted case insensitively

### Styles/StylePersistencePolicyTests.cs

_StylePersistencePolicy is the pure half of the v1.34.3 fix for a player report: the chat-picked narrative style reset to "Default" on every game restart and every mod update, because the mod's StyleService held the selection only in a static field with no persistence. The fix writes the chosen NAME to a small file in the player's Bannerlord user data and restores it at startup; this policy turns the raw file bytes (which may be missing, blank, multi-line, or hand-edited into a stale name) into the canonical style name to restore, validated against the styles actually on disk. Get it wrong and either the persisted choice is lost anyway (the bug returns) or a corrupt/stale file blanks the voice or resurrects a deleted style._

- GIVEN a persisted name still on disk, WHEN resolving, THEN it is restored canonically
- GIVEN no persistence file, WHEN resolving, THEN the default is used
- GIVEN a blank persisted file, WHEN resolving, THEN the default is used
- GIVEN a persisted name no longer on disk, WHEN resolving, THEN the default is used
- GIVEN a file with a trailing newline or extra lines, WHEN resolving, THEN only the first line is the name
- GIVEN a chosen name, WHEN serialized and resolved back, THEN the same name is restored
- GIVEN a blank name, WHEN serialized, THEN it collapses to the default

## Tournaments

### Tournaments/TournamentMemoryPolicyTests.cs

_TournamentMemoryPolicy turns a read arena bracket into what each lord carries away: their decisive relation to the player (they beat the player, the player beat them, or they merely co-competed / took the crown), the tone they speak of it in (brag / gracious / respect / sore), and whether it warms into an appreciation or curdles into a grudge. This is the whole point of the pillar: a lord must approach about the RIGHT result in a register true to his character. Get Classify wrong and a lord brags about beating a player he actually lost to; get Tone/Aftermath wrong and every result reads the same, so the sore loser and the worthy rival (Gabriel wanted both, by traits) both collapse into bland politeness. These pin the boundaries the LLM prompt and the appreciation/grudge feeders will trust._

- GIVEN a hero the player beat, WHEN classified, THEN they lost to the player
- GIVEN a hero who put the player out, WHEN classified, THEN they beat the player
- GIVEN a champion who never faced the player, WHEN classified, THEN they are a champion co competitor
- GIVEN an unrelated participant, WHEN classified, THEN there is no relation
- GIVEN a proud low honour victor, WHEN toned, THEN they brag
- GIVEN an honourable victor, WHEN toned, THEN they are gracious
- GIVEN a proud low honour loser, WHEN toned, THEN they are sore
- GIVEN an honourable loser, WHEN toned, THEN they are gracious
- GIVEN a bragging champion co competitor, WHEN toned, THEN they brag
- GIVEN a tone, WHEN resolving aftermath, THEN it maps to the right lasting feeling
- GIVEN null inputs, WHEN toned, THEN it is measured and does not throw

### Tournaments/TournamentRivalryPolicyTests.cs

_TournamentRivalryPolicy decides when repeated arena meetings become a standing rivalry and of which flavor. It is what makes Gabriel's "both, by traits" real at the rivalry level: the same tally of head-to-heads becomes a warm worthy-rivalry or a sour grudge purely from the lord's honour. Get the threshold wrong and rivalries form on a single meeting (every opponent a rival) or never (the feature is dead); get the flavor wrong and a gracious knight nurses a grudge, or a bitter cheat is recorded as a friend._

- GIVEN too few meetings, WHEN evaluated, THEN there is no rivalry
- GIVEN enough meetings and an honourable lord, WHEN evaluated, THEN the rivalry is friendly
- GIVEN enough meetings and a low honour lord, WHEN evaluated, THEN the rivalry is bitter
- GIVEN exactly the threshold, WHEN evaluated, THEN a rivalry forms
- GIVEN null traits at threshold, WHEN evaluated, THEN it is friendly and does not throw
- GIVEN an unforgiving rival, WHEN a bitter rivalry plants its grudge, THEN it clears the formation floor
- GIVEN a merciful rival, WHEN a bitter rivalry plants its grudge, THEN mercy forgives it

## Violence

### Violence/ViolenceConsequencePolicyTests.cs

_ViolenceConsequencePolicy prices what an act of violence costs the player, and it is the half of the pillar that keeps the sandbox honest: the reporter who asked for this feature said himself he expected it could be "pretty OP". Three failures would each open an exploit. If a deed done in private cost nothing, the optimal play would be to get every victim alone and cut throats for free. If a FAILED attempt cost nothing, attempted murder would be a free roll to retry forever. And if the tiers flattened, robbing a merchant would weigh the same with the law as murdering a lord. These pin the shape of the price, not merely its size._

- GIVEN a forbidden act, WHEN priced, THEN it costs nothing
- GIVEN nobody watching, WHEN priced, THEN the law still hears of it
- GIVEN witnesses, WHEN priced, THEN the crime grows with who saw it
- GIVEN a deed done unseen, WHEN priced, THEN no standing is lost but it is when seen
- GIVEN a failed attempt, WHEN priced, THEN it still costs but less than the deed
- GIVEN the three acts, WHEN priced, THEN killing outweighs beating outweighs robbery
- GIVEN a killing, WHEN priced, THEN the dead lose no regard but a survivor hates you
- GIVEN the three acts, WHEN priced, THEN only blood and beatings make enemies of kin

### Violence/ViolencePolicyTests.cs

_ViolencePolicy is the anti-exploit spine of player-inflicted violence (player request 2026-09-06). It answers three things: may this be attempted at all, how likely is it, and how did this attempt end. Four failures would each break the feature in a different way. If the lethal gate leaked, a campaign with the MCM toggle OFF could still lose characters to a knife, which is the one thing Gabriel ruled must never happen by default. If the odds ignored the victim's company, the sandbox-breaking case the reporter himself worried about (walking up to the emperor and cutting his throat) would be as easy as mugging a lone villager. If certainty crept in at either end, the feature would become either a guaranteed power fantasy or a dead button. And if Resolve were not deterministic on its roll, none of it could be tested at all._

- GIVEN lethal violence is disabled, WHEN checking permission, THEN only killing is barred
- GIVEN lethal violence is disabled, WHEN resolving a kill, THEN it is forbidden whatever the roll
- GIVEN lethal violence is enabled, WHEN resolving a kill, THEN it resolves on the roll
- GIVEN an even match alone, WHEN robbing, THEN the odds are even
- GIVEN the victim has company, WHEN attacking, THEN it is strictly harder and guards worst
- GIVEN a weak player against a guarded champion, WHEN killing, THEN the odds fall to the floor but never to zero
- GIVEN the same victim, WHEN comparing acts, THEN robbery beats assault beats killing
- GIVEN a practised rogue, WHEN robbing, THEN the craft helps but not when killing
- GIVEN overwhelming odds, WHEN attacking, THEN it is capped short of certainty
- GIVEN a roll at the boundary, WHEN resolving, THEN below succeeds and at the value fails
- GIVEN the three acts, WHEN asking who needs confirming, THEN only killing does
- GIVEN missing facts, WHEN asking the odds, THEN it floors and does not throw

### Violence/ViolenceRumorTests.cs

_Player violence Inc 4: whether a deed becomes news, and the words the realm uses for it. Two things are pinned here. The CIRCULATION rule decides what the world may confront the player about, and it carries the pillar's balance: a murder must travel even when nobody watched (a body is found, a name is missed), or the optimal play would be to isolate every victim and kill in silence forever; while a robbery nobody saw must NOT become realm news, or the map would gossip about every mugging and the feature would read as noise. The WORDING guards the same truth rule as the battle pillar: the realm speaks as hearsay, and only a real killing is spoken of as a death, because these lines are fed straight into a prompt._

- GIVEN a murder nobody saw, WHEN priced, THEN word still travels
- GIVEN an unseen robbery or beating, WHEN priced, THEN it does not become news
- GIVEN witnesses, WHEN priced, THEN a robbery or beating becomes news
- GIVEN a murder against a public beating, WHEN priced, THEN the murder carries further
- GIVEN a failed attempt, WHEN priced, THEN nothing circulates
- GIVEN a deed that travels, WHEN worded, THEN the realm speaks as hearsay
- GIVEN a beating or robbery, WHEN worded, THEN no death is claimed
- GIVEN a bystander, WHEN worded, THEN they speak first hand
- GIVEN no place, WHEN worded, THEN the line stays clean

## WorldAlmanac

### WorldAlmanac/PowerIndexPolicyTests.cs

_World Almanac, the Power Index. This policy IS the dashboard's brain: the whole "Balance of Power" screen is just a rendering of what it returns, and Gabriel tunes the weights against a real campaign through cr.powerindex before any UI is built, so the numbers have to behave predictably. What matters is not the exact magic constants (those are knobs) but the SHAPE: more of a pillar scores higher, the field leader sits in the low 90s rather than a flat 100 (the headroom that lets dominance still grow), a pillar the field cannot yet tell apart (war record in V1, before the battle ledger) collapses to a neutral midpoint instead of dragging everyone to zero, the ranking follows the index, and the key indicators point at the true single-measure leaders. Get the normalization wrong and the panel either crowns everyone equally or zeroes the weak._

- GIVEN two kingdoms of different strength, WHEN scoring, THEN the leader tops the pillar and the other is a proportional share
- GIVEN a clear leader, WHEN scoring, THEN its pillar stays below one hundred
- GIVEN a field, WHEN scoring, THEN it is ranked by power index descending
- GIVEN the pillar weights, WHEN summed, THEN they make a whole
- GIVEN no war data in the field, WHEN scoring, THEN every war pillar is neutral
- GIVEN a real war record, WHEN scoring, THEN the victor beats the loser on the war pillar
- GIVEN a field, WHEN scoring, THEN the key indicators point at each single measure leader
- GIVEN a field, WHEN scoring, THEN the totals sum across all kingdoms
- GIVEN any field, WHEN scoring, THEN every power index is within zero and one hundred
- GIVEN a single kingdom, WHEN scoring, THEN it ranks first at the headroom ceiling
- GIVEN no field, WHEN scoring, THEN the result is empty

### WorldAlmanac/SeasonalTurnPolicyTests.cs

_that a season which changed nothing is not recorded, and that one which did is described the way a person would remark on it rather than the way a ledger would._

- GIVEN a season in which nothing much moved, WHEN summarised, THEN nothing is recorded
- GIVEN a stronghold changed hands, WHEN summarised, THEN it is history whatever else held steady
- GIVEN a realm that grew rich, WHEN summarised, THEN that is what it says
- GIVEN a realm in decline, WHEN summarised, THEN the decline is recorded too
- GIVEN a small realm and a great one, WHEN each gains the same coin, THEN only the small one is news
- GIVEN several things moved, WHEN summarised, THEN it reads as a chronicler would write it
- GIVEN no previous standing, WHEN summarised, THEN nothing is claimed
- GIVEN any recorded turn, WHEN summarised, THEN it says when
